### From GM crops to genome-edited crops
## 1. News
- GE rice varieties: improved Samba Mahsuri and MTU-1010; canola-quality low-pungency mustard (2nd year trials, possible release 2026)
- ⚠ Only commercial GM crop in India: Bt cotton
| Variety | Edit / benefit |
| Samba Mahsuri (GE) | Gn1a (cytokinin oxidase) edited → more spikelets, ~19% yield gain |
| MTU-1010 (GE) | Drought/salt tolerance — saline/alkaline soils |
| Mustard (GE) | Low pungency, disease/pest resistance |
## 2. Regulation and funding
- March 2022 MoEFCC OM: transgene-free GE plants exempt from GM biosafety rules — need IBSC certification, ⚠ not GEAC
- Funding: National Agricultural Science Fund (~2018); ₹500 crore in Union Budget 2023–24
- ICAR mapped ~178 genes in 24 field crops and 43 genes in horticultural crops
## 3. Tools
- Cas9, Cas12a; newer GeoCas9, CasLambda supplied by Innovative Genomics Institute (founded by Jennifer Doudna) — training at IARI (Feb 2025)
- Indigenous TnpB-based mini editor: team led by Kutubuddin Ali Molla, Central Rice Research Institute (CRRI), Cuttack
- Training: 9 scientists trained abroad (US, Europe, Australia, CIMMYT Mexico); 12 more scheduled
- GM vs GE: GM inserts foreign genes (transgenic); GE edits own genes, no foreign DNA
### ANRF launches SARAL AI tool
## 1. News
- SARAL: Simplified and Automated Research Amplification and Learning — launched by Anusandhan National Research Foundation (ANRF)
- Purpose: AI (NLP) generates layperson-friendly summaries of research papers — short videos, podcasts, posters, presentations
## 2. Background
- ANRF: India's newest science funding agency; ~70% budget from private sources
- RDI Scheme: ₹1 lakh crore Research Development and Innovation Scheme approved by Cabinet in July 2025 — low-interest, long-tenure loans to private firms

## 1. IMC 2025

| Component | Provider |
| Radio Access Network (RAN) | Tejas Networks |
| Core Network | C-DOT |
| Integration & data centres | TCS — 100,000+ sites; Cognitive Network Operations platform |
- ⚠ India joins Denmark, Sweden, South Korea, China as makers of own 4G equipment
- 5G-ready; export potential vs China's Digital Silk Road (Huawei, ZTE ~70% of Africa's 4G)
| Feature | 4G | 5G | 6G |
| Launch | 2009–2012 | 2019 onwards | 2030+ |
| Peak speed | 100 Mbps mobile / 1 Gbps | 10–20 Gbps | 1 Tbps+ |
| Latency | ~50 ms | ~1–10 ms | ~0.1 ms |
| Key tech | LTE, OFDMA, MIMO | mmWave, Massive MIMO, network slicing | Terahertz, AI-native |

- Context: India ~1 billion internet users; petition by Abhishek Bachchan and Aishwarya Rai Bachchan against AI videos

- Follow-on of: Project 17 (Shivalik-class)
- Total: 7 ships — 4 MDL, 3 GRSE; INS Nilgiri first (January 2025); remaining 4 within 1.5 years
- Indigenous content: ~75%, 200+ MSMEs
- Next: Project 17B — 7 next-gen frigates
| Spec | Detail |
| Length / displacement | 149 m / ~6,670 tonnes |
| Speed | 28 knots |
| Weapons | 8 BrahMos VLS, LRSAM/Barak 8, torpedoes, rocket launchers, 127 mm gun, 2 AK-630 CIWS |
| Sensors | Humsa (NG) sonar; 'Shakti' EW suite |
## 3. Names and commands
| Ship | Named after | Command | Motto |
| INS Udaygiri | mountain range in Andhra Pradesh | Eastern Naval Command (Sunrise Fleet) | Sanyuktaha Paramojayaha |
| INS Himgiri | Himalayas | Western Naval Command | Adrushyam Ajayam |
- Legacy Udaygiri (1976–2007): Op Pawan (1987), Op Cactus (1988), Op Madad (1991), Op Castor (2005 tsunami)
- Legacy Himgiri (1974–2005): Bombay High patrol (1985), Op Cactus, Gujarat quake relief (2001)

### DRDO tests Integrated Air Defence Weapon System (IADWS)
## 1. News
- Date: 23 August 2025, off Odisha coast — maiden flight tests
- Integrates: QRSAM (Quick Reaction SAM), VSHORADS (very short range, shoulder-fired), Directed Energy Weapon (DEW) — high-power laser
- Control: Centralised Command and Control Centre by DRDL
- Result: three targets — two fixed-wing UAVs + one multi-copter drone — destroyed simultaneously
- Range: Chandipur Integrated Test Range (ITR)
- Labs: DRDL, Research Centre Imarat, Centre for High Energy Systems and Sciences (CHESS)
- ⚠ Fully indigenous — not with Israel's Rafael
## 2. India's air defence layers
| Layer | Systems |
| Long | Barak-8, S-400 |
| Medium | Akash, MR-SAM |
| Short | QRSAM, Spyder |
| Very short | VSHORADS, MANPADS |
### India–France (Safran) 120 kN engine for AMCA
## 1. News
- Deal: Government-to-Government — co-develop 120 kN jet engine with Safran (France)
- For: AMCA Mk-2 (Tranche 2); AMCA Mk-1 uses GE-414 (USA)
- Development time: 10 years
- MRO: Safran's Hyderabad facility for Rafale M88 engine — ⚠ first M88 hub outside France
- Framework: Horizon 2047 roadmap; 2026 — India–France Year of Innovation
## 2. Background
- AMCA: indigenous 5th-generation fighter by Aeronautical Development Agency (ADA); induction by 2035
- AMCA (May 2026): ADA (DRDO); 5th-generation twin-engine stealth fighter; flight-test centre at Puttaparthi (AP)
- India–France: 36 Rafale; Scorpene submarines; ISRO–CNES
### ISRO Integrated Air Drop Test (IADT-1) for Gaganyaan
## 1. News
- Test: ISRO's first Integrated Air Drop Test (IADT-1) — validated parachute-based deceleration system of Gaganyaan crew module
- How: ~5-tonne dummy crew capsule dropped from an IAF Chinook helicopter; main parachutes deployed in sequence
- ⚠ Not an orbital flight — uncrewed orbital flights are G1 onwards
## 2. Gaganyaan progress (MoS Jitendra Singh)
- Done: propulsion systems, ECLSS engineering model, Crew Escape System — five types of motors, TV-D1 precursor; HS200 motors; C32-G stage
- Next: TV-D2, G1 (uncrewed)

| Bharatiya Antariksh Station | 2035 |
| Indian Moon landing | 2040 |
### ISRO's second launch complex at Kulasekarapattinam
## 1. News
- Where: Kulasekarapattinam, Tuticorin (Thoothukudi) district, Tamil Nadu — ~2,300 acres
- For: SSLV (Small Satellite Launch Vehicle) — ⚠ not PSLV/GSLV (they stay at Sriharikota, AP)

### National Space Day and need for a space law
## 1. News
- Second National Space Day: 23 August 2025 — marks Chandrayaan-3 soft landing near lunar south pole
- Upcoming: Gaganyaan, Chandrayaan-4, Bharat Antariksh Station
- Gap: India has ratified UN space treaties but no comprehensive national space law
- Still no Space Activities Act (NextGen S&T CA 2026)
- ⚠ IN-SPACe works without statutory authority
## 2. Existing framework
- Catalogue of Indian Standards for Space Industry — safety
- Indian Space Policy (2023)
- IN-SPACe Norms, Guidelines and Procedures (NPG), 2023 — authorisation of commercial activities
- Outer Space Treaty (1967): space the "province of all mankind"; Article VI — state responsible for private activities; treaties not self-executing
- Countries with laws: Japan, Luxembourg, US
- Persons: Rossana Deim-Hoffmann (UNOOSA Global Space Law Project Lead); space law expert Ranjana Kaul; T.H. Anand Rao (Indian Space Association)
### UNGA launches Scientific Panel on AI and Global Dialogue on AI Governance
## 1. News
- Bodies: UN Independent International Scientific Panel on AI (annual reports) and Global Dialogue on AI Governance
- Dialogue sessions: July 2026, Geneva; 2027, New York
- Linked to: Global Digital Compact (September 2024) under the Pact for the Future
- UN SG Antonio Guterres: "pathbreaking milestone"
### IISc study — Asgard archaea with two FtsZ genes
## 1. News
- By: IISc Bengaluru — in The EMBO Journal
- Organism: Odinarchaeota (an Asgard archaea) — has two FtsZ genes (OdinFtsZ1, OdinFtsZ2) plus a tubulin-like gene
- OdinFtsZ1: straight protofilaments, anchors directly to membranes (bacteria-like)
- OdinFtsZ2: spiral ring-like structures, needs adaptor proteins
- Methods: phylogenetics, cryo-electron microscopy
## 2. Background
- Asgard archaea: named after Norse gods (Odin-, Loki-, Thorarchaeota); found in deep-sea sediments; closest living relatives of eukaryotes
- FtsZ: bacterial division protein forming the Z-ring; tubulin is the eukaryotic hallmark
- ⚠ Tubulin is not unique to bacteria — it is eukaryotic
### Brain-eating amoeba (PAM) cases in Kerala
## 1. News
- Disease: Primary Amoebic Meningoencephalitis (PAM) — caused by Naegleria fowleri
- 2025: three cases in Kozhikode district — death of a nine-year-old, a three-month-old infected
- Kerala fatality: ~25% vs global ~97% (early diagnosis)
| Year | Milestone |
| 1971 | First Indian case |
| 2016 | Kerala's first case — Alappuzha |
| 2024 | 14-year-old boy in Kozhikode — first Indian to survive PAM; only 11 survivors globally |
## 2. Background
- Route: water through the nose → olfactory nerve → brain
- ⚠ Not communicable; not spread by drinking contaminated water
### US first human case of New World screwworm
## 1. News
- Confirmed: 24 August 2025 by US HHS and CDC — traveller returning from El Salvador; infestation called myiasis
## 2. Background
- US eradication: 1966 via Sterile Insect Technique (SIT); later Mexico (1970s), Central America (2000s); Florida outbreak 2017
### Rare quadruple star system UPM J1040−3551 AabBab
## 1. News
- System: two cold T-type brown dwarfs orbiting a pair of young red dwarfs — ⚠ first such arrangement seen
- Journal: Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society (Sept 2025)
## 2. Background
- Brown dwarfs: "failed stars" — cannot sustain hydrogen fusion; up to 70 times Jupiter's mass; companion chance <5%
- Red dwarfs: most common stars — ~70% of Milky Way stars; long-lived
- ⚠ Blue supergiants are among the hottest, most luminous stars — not cold
### Entangled atomic clocks to probe quantum–gravity interface
## 1. News
- Study: Jacob Covey, Igor Pikovski, Johannes Borregaard — in PRX Quantum (July 2025)
- Proposal: tabletop experiment with three entangled ytterbium atomic clocks at different elevations (km-scale)
- Entanglement: W state (robust to particle loss, unlike GHZ state)
- Expected: frequency shift ~0.02 Hz for 1 km separation; coherence time ~50 seconds
## 2. Background
- Quantum gravity theories: String Theory (extra dimensions, gravitons), Loop Quantum Gravity (discrete spacetime at Planck scale)

### CAR T-cell therapy for cancer
## 1. Basics
- CAR: Chimeric Antigen Receptor — patient's own T-cells (⚠ not B-cells) engineered to attack cancer
- Approved for blood cancers: ALL, Non-Hodgkin Lymphoma, Multiple Myeloma — ⚠ not yet solid tumours
- Side effects: Cytokine Release Syndrome (CRS), ICANS (neurotoxicity)
- Cost abroad: over ₹3–4 crore per patient
## 2. India
- IIT Bombay + Tata Memorial Centre — India's first cost-effective platform 'InCART'
- DBT supports Make-in-India CAR-T
- NexCAR19: India's first indigenous CAR-T therapy (blood cancer); CDSCO approval 2023 (CDI)
- Dual-targeting CAR-T (BCMA + CD19): co-developed with the Technology Development Board; multiple myeloma (LENSD, 18 May 2026)
- ⚠ NexCAR19 = first CAR-T (blood cancer); BIRSA 101 = first CRISPR therapy (sickle cell)
### AI and the news industry — copyright concerns
## 1. News
- Issue: LLMs trained on news content without consent or compensation
- India: DPIIT formed a committee on copyright and AI
- Precedents: Australia and Canada laws making tech giants pay publishers
- ⚠ "Fair use" does not mandate compensation — allows limited use without permission

### Semicon India 2025 — Vikram 32-bit processor
## 1. News
- Date: 2 September 2025 — Ashwini Vaishnaw presented PM Modi the 'Made in India' Vikram 32-bit launch-vehicle-grade processor
- Developed by: Vikram Sarabhai Space Centre (ISRO) + Semiconductor Laboratory (SCL), Chandigarh
- Vikram 3201 (2025): ISRO's first indigenous 32-bit space-grade microprocessor, along with Kalpana32
- VIKRAM3201/KALPANA3201 from SCL Chandigarh
- Predecessor: VIKRAM1601 16-bit — used in ISRO avionics since 2009
- ⚠ Not developed under Semicon India Programme — builds on ISRO's earlier work
- Also shown: 31 prototype chips from IIT Jammu, IIT Roorkee, IIT Dhanbad, IIT Ropar, NIT Durgapur, NIT Calicut
## 2. India Semiconductor Mission
- Launched: December 2021 under MeitY
- Status: 5 fab units under construction; pilot line of one done; 2 more to start soon
- Global market: $1 trillion by 2030–35; India has 20% of world's chip design engineers
### IASV Triveni — first tri-service all-women circumnavigation
## 1. News
- Start: 11 September 2025 — ⚠ first-ever tri-service all-women sailing circumnavigation
- Vessel: Indian Army Sailing Vessel (IASV) Triveni — 50-foot yacht built at Indian Naval Dockyard, Puducherry (⚠ not Goa)
- Crew: 10 women officers — 5 Army, 1 Navy, 4 Air Force
- Leader: Lt Col Anuja Varudkar; Sqn Ldr Shraddha Raju (Su-30 technical officer); Lt Cdr Priyanka Gusain
- Distance: 21,600 nautical miles, 8–9 months; return to Mumbai by May 2026
- Route: cross equator twice; three Great Capes — Cape Leeuwin (Australia), Cape Horn (S. America), Cape of Good Hope (S. Africa); hardest — Drake Passage
- Ports: Australia, New Zealand, Argentina, South Africa
### Exercise Yudh Abhyas 2025 — India–US, Alaska
## 1. News
- Edition: 21st; Fort Wainwright, Alaska; 1–14 September 2025
- India: battalion of Madras Regiment
- US: 1st Battalion, 5th Infantry Regiment 'Bobcats' — Arctic Wolves Brigade Combat Team, 11th Airborne Division
- Focus: high-altitude/mountain warfare, heliborne ops, UAS and counter-UAS, UN peacekeeping
- Started: 2004; annual, alternating venues
## 2. India–US defence background
| Agreement | Year |
| LEMOA (logistics) | 2016 |
| COMCASA (communications) | 2018 |
| BECA (geospatial) | 2020 |
| SOSA (Security of Supply) | 2024 |
- USS Frank Cable visited Chennai in August 2025
- Procurements: 24 MH-60R helicopters; 145 M777 howitzers; Sig Sauer 716 rifles; 31 MQ-9B drones (Oct 2024, ~₹28,000 crore); GE F-414 engines for LCA Mk-2 (80% ToT); GE F-404 for Tejas Mk-1A
### Project 75(I) — MDL–TKMS submarine contract talks
## 1. News
- Negotiations: Mazagon Dock Shipbuilders Ltd (MDL), Mumbai, began contract talks for Project 75(I) with ThyssenKrupp Marine Systems (TKMS), Germany
- Scope: six conventional submarines with Air-Independent Propulsion (AIP); technology transfer
- TKMS known for: Type 214, Type 218 submarines
- MDL earlier: Scorpene-class under Project 75 with France
- India–Germany Defence Cooperation Agreement: 2019
### ESA Solar Orbiter traces origin of solar energetic electrons
## 1. News
- Mission: ESA–NASA Solar Orbiter, launched February 2020
- Finding: first to trace Solar Energetic Electrons (SEE) to solar flares (bursts) and CMEs (gradual release); 300+ SEE bursts studied (2020–2022)
- ⚠ First spacecraft to image the Sun's poles
## 2. Related missions
| Mission | Detail |
| Parker Solar Probe (NASA, 2018) | Closest approach to Sun |
| Aditya-L1 (ISRO, 2023) | India's first solar observatory at Lagrange Point L1 |
- Solar cycle: 11-year magnetic cycle
### WHO endorses GLP-1 weight-loss drugs
## 1. News
- WHO: for the first time endorsed GLP-1 receptor agonists for obesity
- Drugs: Semaglutide — Ozempic (diabetes), Wegovy (weight loss); Tirzepatide — Mounjaro, Zepbound
- Generics: Dr. Reddy's, Cipla, Biocon preparing biosimilars after patent expiry (~2025-26)
- Side effects: gastroparesis, pancreatitis, thyroid cancer risk
## 2. Data
- World (2022): 1 in 8 adults obese — 890 million adults, 160 million adolescents
### Adult neurogenesis confirmed in hippocampus
- ★ Hypothalamus — basal part of the diencephalon (forebrain)
### INS Udaygiri and INS Himgiri commissioned
## 1. News
- Date: 26 August 2025 at Visakhapatnam — Nilgiri-class (Project 17A) stealth guided-missile frigates
- ⚠ First time two major warships from two different shipyards commissioned together — Udaygiri: MDL Mumbai; Himgiri: GRSE Kolkata
- Milestone: Udaygiri is the 100th ship designed by Navy's Warship Design Bureau
## 2. Project 17A
- Follow-on of: Project 17 (Shivalik-class)
- Total: 7 ships — 4 MDL, 3 GRSE; INS Nilgiri first (January 2025); remaining 4 within 1.5 years
- Indigenous content: ~75%, 200+ MSMEs
- Next: Project 17B — 7 next-gen frigates
| Spec | Detail |
| Length / displacement | 149 m / ~6,670 tonnes |
| Speed | 28 knots |
| Weapons | 8 BrahMos VLS, LRSAM/Barak 8, torpedoes, rocket launchers, 127 mm gun, 2 AK-630 CIWS |
| Sensors | Humsa (NG) sonar; 'Shakti' EW suite |
## 3. Names and commands
| Ship | Named after | Command | Motto |
| INS Udaygiri | mountain range in Andhra Pradesh | Eastern Naval Command (Sunrise Fleet) | Sanyuktaha Paramojayaha |
| INS Himgiri | Himalayas | Western Naval Command | Adrushyam Ajayam |
- Legacy Udaygiri (1976–2007): Op Pawan (1987), Op Cactus (1988), Op Madad (1991), Op Castor (2005 tsunami)
- Legacy Himgiri (1974–2005): Bombay High patrol (1985), Op Cactus, Gujarat quake relief (2001)
- 2025 commissionings also: INS Surat (destroyer), INS Vaghsheer (submarine), INS Arnala (ASW craft), INS Nistar (diving support vessel)
### DRDO tests Integrated Air Defence Weapon System (IADWS)
## 1. News
- Date: 23 August 2025, off Odisha coast — maiden flight tests
- Integrates: QRSAM (Quick Reaction SAM), VSHORADS (very short range, shoulder-fired), Directed Energy Weapon (DEW) — high-power laser
- Control: Centralised Command and Control Centre by DRDL
- Result: three targets — two fixed-wing UAVs + one multi-copter drone — destroyed simultaneously
- Range: Chandipur Integrated Test Range (ITR)
- Labs: DRDL, Research Centre Imarat, Centre for High Energy Systems and Sciences (CHESS)
- ⚠ Fully indigenous — not with Israel's Rafael
## 2. India's air defence layers
| Layer | Systems |
| Long | Barak-8, S-400 |
| Medium | Akash, MR-SAM |
| Short | QRSAM, Spyder |
| Very short | VSHORADS, MANPADS |
### ISRO Integrated Air Drop Test (IADT-1) for Gaganyaan
## 1. News
- Test: ISRO's first Integrated Air Drop Test (IADT-1) — validated parachute-based deceleration system of Gaganyaan crew module
- How: ~5-tonne dummy crew capsule dropped from an IAF Chinook helicopter; main parachutes deployed in sequence
- ⚠ Not an orbital flight — uncrewed orbital flights are G1 onwards
## 2. Gaganyaan progress (MoS Jitendra Singh)
- Done: propulsion systems, ECLSS engineering model, Crew Escape System — five types of motors, TV-D1 precursor; HS200 motors; C32-G stage
- Next: TV-D2, G1 (uncrewed)
| Milestone | Target |
| Crewed flight | Q1 2027 |
| Bharatiya Antariksh Station | 2035 |
| Indian Moon landing | 2040 |
### National Space Day and need for a space law
## 1. News
- Second National Space Day: 23 August 2025 — marks Chandrayaan-3 soft landing near lunar south pole
- Upcoming: Gaganyaan, Chandrayaan-4, Bharat Antariksh Station
- Gap: India has ratified UN space treaties but no comprehensive national space law
- Still no Space Activities Act (NextGen S&T CA 2026)
- ⚠ IN-SPACe works without statutory authority
## 2. Existing framework
- Catalogue of Indian Standards for Space Industry — safety
- Indian Space Policy (2023)
- IN-SPACe Norms, Guidelines and Procedures (NPG), 2023 — authorisation of commercial activities
- Outer Space Treaty (1967): space the "province of all mankind"; Article VI — state responsible for private activities; treaties not self-executing
- Countries with laws: Japan, Luxembourg, US
- Persons: Rossana Deim-Hoffmann (UNOOSA Global Space Law Project Lead); space law expert Ranjana Kaul; T.H. Anand Rao (Indian Space Association)
### UNGA launches Scientific Panel on AI and Global Dialogue on AI Governance
- UNGA launched UN Independent International Scientific Panel on AI and Global Dialogue on AI Governance; linked to Global Digital Compact

- OdinFtsZ1 — attaches directly to membranes
- OdinFtsZ2 — spiral ring-like structures; needs adaptor proteins
### US first human case of New World screwworm
- US eradicated New World screwworm in 1966 — Sterile Insect Technique; Florida outbreak 2017
### Rare quadruple star system UPM J1040−3551 AabBab
- Brown dwarfs — "failed stars"; cannot sustain hydrogen fusion; up to 70 times Jupiter's mass
### Entangled atomic clocks to probe quantum–gravity interface
- String Theory — extra dimensions and gravitons
- Loop Quantum Gravity — discrete spacetime at the Planck scale

- Australia and Canada — laws making tech giants pay news publishers
- Fair use — limited use without permission; does not require payment

- Dates: 14–24 February 2026, Visakhapatnam
- Invited: over 145 countries
